# EK2 Loincloths: who wears a loincloth. Written by source/tools/gen_script_files.py.
# Priority 101 applies this after every EK2 outfit group (EK2's highest is 100), so a
# loincloth wearer never gets an EK2 outfit, legwear or cloak on top of the loincloth.
# Every modifier sets all of our genes, so nothing is left over from another choice.
ek2lc_loincloth = {
	usage = game
	selection_behavior = weighted_random
	priority = 101

	ek2lc_none = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_loincloth
				template = ek2lc_no_loincloth
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_top
				template = ek2lc_no_top
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_war
				template = ek2lc_no_war
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_mantle
				template = ek2lc_no_mantle
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0  # the loincloth cultures' people aged 4 and up always get one of the looks below (2026-11-07)
				has_game_rule = ek2lc_frequency_always  # game rule (2026-11-09): or often, or sometimes (below)
				scope:age >= 4
				OR = {
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_nedic_reach_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_reach
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_ashlander_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_ashlander
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_bosmeri_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_bosmer
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_argonian_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_argonian
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_anequinan_clothing_trigger = yes
						portrait_ek_pellitinian_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_khajiit
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_orc_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_orc
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_goblin_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_goblin
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_nedic_south_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_kothringi
					}
					OR = {
						portrait_ek_lilmothiit_clothing_trigger = yes
						has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_lilmothiit
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 49  # "often": two in three wear a loincloth
				has_game_rule = ek2lc_frequency_often
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 199  # "sometimes": one in three
				has_game_rule = ek2lc_frequency_sometimes
			}
		}
	}

	# Reach men (and boys 7+) who are commoners. EK2's commoner rule already leaves out armour.
	ek2lc_reach_hide_m = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			morph = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_shrink_body
				template = shrink_all
				value = 0
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = no_clothes
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = legwear
				template = no_legwear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = cloaks
				template = no_cloak
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = headgear
				template = no_headgear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = secondary_headgears
				template = no_headgear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = additive_headgear
				template = no_additive
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes_hoods
				template = no_clothes_hood
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_loincloth
				template = ek2lc_reach_hide
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_top
				template = ek2lc_no_top
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_war
				template = ek2lc_no_war
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_mantle
				template = ek2lc_no_mantle
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				is_female = no
				scope:age >= 7
				portrait_commoner_clothing_trigger = {
					CULTURE_INPUT = ek_nedic_reach
				}
				ek2lc_royal_trigger = no
				ek2lc_war_role_trigger = no
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0  # EK2's undead: the lich twin below, or the grave wrap
				ek2lc_undead_look_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

				}
			}
		}

	# Reach men (and boys 7+) who are neither commoners nor rulers: nobles, and knights and court officers when not at war (2026-11-07: until then EK2 dressed them): the loincloth in the finer dyed hide.
	ek2lc_reach_noble_m = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			morph = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_shrink_body
				template = shrink_all
				value = 0
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = no_clothes
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = legwear
				template = no_legwear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = cloaks
				template = no_cloak
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = headgear
				template = no_headgear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = secondary_headgears
				template = no_headgear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = additive_headgear
				template = no_additive
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes_hoods
				template = no_clothes_hood
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_loincloth
				template = ek2lc_reach_fine
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_top
				template = ek2lc_no_top
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_war
				template = ek2lc_no_war
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_mantle
				template = ek2lc_no_mantle
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				is_female = no
				scope:age >= 7
				OR = {
					portrait_ek_nedic_reach_clothing_trigger = yes
					has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_reach
				}
				ek2lc_royal_trigger = no
				ek2lc_war_role_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					portrait_commoner_clothing_trigger = {
						CULTURE_INPUT = ek_nedic_reach
					}
				}
				ek2lc_camp_follower_trigger = no
				has_game_rule = ek2lc_nobles_on
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0  # EK2's undead: the lich twin below, or the grave wrap
				ek2lc_undead_look_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	# Reach men (14+) who follow an army without fighting (ek2lc_camp_follower_trigger): the plain loincloth, under the camp follower's wrap. EK2 dresses an adventurer's camp as low nobility, so the commoner rule alone would miss them.

					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Ashlander women (14+) who follow an army without fighting (ek2lc_camp_follower_trigger): the plain loincloth and the chest band, under the camp follower's wrap. EK2 dresses an adventurer's camp as low nobility, so the commoner rule alone would miss them.
	ek2lc_ashlander_camp_f = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			morph = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_shrink_body
				template = shrink_all
				value = 0
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes
				template = no_clothes
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = legwear
				template = no_legwear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = cloaks
				template = no_cloak
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = headgear
				template = no_headgear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = secondary_headgears
				template = no_headgear
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = additive_headgear
				template = no_additive
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = clothes_hoods
				template = no_clothes_hood
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_loincloth
				template = ek2lc_ashlander_hide
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_top
				template = ek2lc_ashlander_band
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_war
				template = ek2lc_no_war
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_mantle
				template = ek2lc_no_mantle
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				is_female = yes
				scope:age >= 14
				OR = {
					portrait_ek_ashlander_clothing_trigger = yes
					has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_ashlander
				}
				ek2lc_royal_trigger = no
				ek2lc_camp_follower_trigger = yes
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0  # EK2's undead: the lich twin below, or the grave wrap
				ek2lc_undead_look_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_war
				template = ek2lc_no_war
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_mantle
				template = ek2lc_no_mantle
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				is_female = yes
				scope:age >= 14
				OR = {
					portrait_ek_bosmeri_clothing_trigger = yes
					has_character_flag = ek2lc_native_bosmer
				}
				ek2lc_royal_trigger = no
				ek2lc_war_role_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					portrait_commoner_clothing_trigger = {
						CULTURE_INPUT = ek_bosmeri
					}
				}
				ek2lc_camp_follower_trigger = no
				has_game_rule = ek2lc_nobles_on
				ek2lc_lich_look_trigger = yes
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Bosmer women (14+) who follow an army without fighting (ek2lc_camp_follower_trigger): the plain loincloth and the chest band, under the camp follower's wrap. EK2 dresses an adventurer's camp as low nobility, so the commoner rule alone would miss them.

	# Kothringi women (14+) who follow an army without fighting (ek2lc_camp_follower_trigger): the plain loincloth and the chest band, under the camp follower's wrap. EK2 dresses an adventurer's camp as low nobility, so the commoner rule alone would miss them.

				mode = add

				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1000

				ek2lc_grave_look_trigger = no  # a grave wrap hides every extra: don't hand out a hidden one

				OR = {
					has_character_modifier = tournament_wrestling_winner_modifier

				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_wrap
				template = ek2lc_mourning_wrap
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1000
				ek2lc_culture_trigger = yes  # only our wearers' cultures: others keep the entity-less "no" piece
				ek2lc_grave_look_trigger = no  # a grave wrap hides every extra: don't hand out a hidden one
				scope:age >= 14
				ek2lc_wrap_mourning_trigger = yes
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

# gene_ek2lc_cowrie: drawn only over one of our loincloths (see EXTRA in gen_script_files.py).
ek2lc_cowrie = {
	usage = game
	selection_behavior = weighted_random
	priority = 101

	ek2lc_cowrie_none = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_cowrie
				template = ek2lc_no_cowrie
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 1
		}
	}

	ek2lc_cowrie_1 = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_cowrie
				template = ek2lc_cowrie_1
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1000
				ek2lc_culture_trigger = yes  # only our wearers' cultures: others keep the entity-less "no" piece
				ek2lc_grave_look_trigger = no  # a grave wrap hides every extra: don't hand out a hidden one
				scope:age >= 14
				has_variable = ek2lc_battles_won
				var:ek2lc_battles_won >= 3
				var:ek2lc_battles_won < 6
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ek2lc_cowrie_2 = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_cowrie
				template = ek2lc_cowrie_2
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1000
				ek2lc_culture_trigger = yes  # only our wearers' cultures: others keep the entity-less "no" piece
				ek2lc_grave_look_trigger = no  # a grave wrap hides every extra: don't hand out a hidden one
				scope:age >= 14
				has_variable = ek2lc_battles_won
				var:ek2lc_battles_won >= 6
				var:ek2lc_battles_won < 10
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ek2lc_cowrie_3 = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_cowrie
				template = ek2lc_cowrie_3
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1000
				ek2lc_culture_trigger = yes  # only our wearers' cultures: others keep the entity-less "no" piece
				ek2lc_grave_look_trigger = no  # a grave wrap hides every extra: don't hand out a hidden one
				scope:age >= 14
				has_variable = ek2lc_battles_won
				var:ek2lc_battles_won >= 10
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

# gene_ek2lc_tabard: drawn only over one of our loincloths (see EXTRA in gen_script_files.py).
ek2lc_tabard = {
	usage = game
	selection_behavior = weighted_random
	priority = 101

	ek2lc_tabard_none = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_tabard
				template = ek2lc_no_tabard
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 1
		}
	}

	ek2lc_holy_tabard = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			accessory = {
				mode = add
				gene = gene_ek2lc_tabard
				template = ek2lc_holy_tabard
				range = { 0 1 }
			}
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1000
				ek2lc_culture_trigger = yes  # only our wearers' cultures: others keep the entity-less "no" piece
				ek2lc_grave_look_trigger = no  # a grave wrap hides every extra: don't hand out a hidden one
				scope:age >= 14
				OR = {
					government_has_flag = government_is_holy_order
					liege ?= { government_has_flag = government_is_holy_order }
				}
				NOR = {
					should_be_fully_naked_portrait_trigger = yes
					should_be_topless_portrait_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

}
